# Skills Lexicon: Claude Sonnet 5.5 edition

Machine drafts by **Claude Sonnet 5.5** (`claude-sonnet-5-5`) for the roster pinned at [`91312fe`](https://github.com/nikbearbrown/skills-lexicon/commit/91312fec957388afe4d1dfbdb4aff2707588b9a1). Not human-reviewed. 2895 of 2895 concepts drafted (0 pending).

| Class | Drafted | Total |
|---|---:|---:|
| skill | 1988 | 1988 |
| ability | 67 | 67 |
| knowledge | 550 | 550 |
| language | 290 | 290 |

Each entry shows its kind, concept ID, definition, basis, and any variants, relations and flags. `basis: model_knowledge` means no permitted source was matched to the exact claim.

## Ability

### Kind: ability

**Adaptability** (`yegnhni99h`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
An enduring tendency and capacity to adjust thinking, behavior and effort when tasks, people, tools or conditions change, while keeping performance steady.

Flags: classification_dispute: Reads as a dispositional work style or personal characteristic rather than an O*NET-type ability; the canonical ability class is preserved for comparison.

**Arm-Hand Steadiness** (`dbywgn8w10`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.2.a.1)  
The ability to keep your hand and arm steady while moving your arm or while holding your arm and hand in one position.

**Attention to detail** (`gdzh66chlx`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
A person's consistent tendency and capacity to notice, check and get right small features of work, such as errors, inconsistencies and exact specifications.

Flags: classification_dispute: A work-style disposition more than an enduring ability; canonical class preserved.

**Auditory Attention** (`gq46lt98ml`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.4.b.2)  
The ability to focus on a single source of sound in the presence of other distracting sounds.

**Category Flexibility** (`zo2ftjz7uz`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.b.7)  
The ability to generate or use different sets of rules for combining or grouping things in different ways.

**Causal reasoning** (`njlcvlg504`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
The cognitive capacity to infer what causes what from evidence, separating cause from mere correlation and anticipating the effects of an intervention.

Flags: sense_note: Can also be trained as a developed reasoning skill (for example through experimental design and statistics), so the ability/skill boundary is arguable; ability class kept for comparison.

**Control Precision** (`qi5340vq2a`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.2.b.1)  
The ability to quickly and repeatedly adjust the controls of a machine or a vehicle to exact positions.

**Deductive Reasoning** (`wclhs8ljcp`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.b.4)  
The ability to apply general rules to specific problems to produce answers that make sense.

**Depth Perception** (`pnlf6la7uc`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.4.a.6)  
The ability to judge which of several objects is closer or farther away from you, or to judge the distance between you and an object.

**Dynamic Flexibility** (`xbjj61um58`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.3.c.2)  
The ability to quickly and repeatedly bend, stretch, twist, or reach out with your body, arms, and/or legs.

**Dynamic Strength** (`iayo0fqvz9`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.3.a.3)  
The ability to exert muscle force repeatedly or continuously over time. This involves muscular endurance and resistance to muscle fatigue.

**Emotional stability** (`ce6c1rs0jk`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
A stable disposition to stay calm, even-tempered and effective under pressure, criticism or setbacks rather than reacting with marked anxiety or mood swings.

Flags: classification_dispute: A personality trait or work style rather than an ability in the O*NET sense; canonical class preserved.

**Explosive Strength** (`qw33m4mbfu`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.3.a.2)  
The ability to use short bursts of muscle force to propel oneself (as in jumping or sprinting), or to throw an object.

**Extent Flexibility** (`zltjecmay2`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.3.c.1)  
The ability to bend, stretch, twist, or reach with your body, arms, and/or legs.

**Far Vision** (`liqhm0notn`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.4.a.2)  
The ability to see details at a distance.

**Finger Dexterity** (`v9gfz4advt`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.2.a.3)  
The ability to make precisely coordinated movements of the fingers of one or both hands to grasp, manipulate, or assemble very small objects.

**Flexibility of Closure** (`jy0dtiz3ve`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.e.2)  
The ability to identify or detect a known pattern (a figure, object, word, or sound) that is hidden in other distracting material.

**Fluency of Ideas** (`pkfc0dzkvm`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.b.1)  
The ability to come up with a number of ideas about a topic (the number of ideas is important, not their quality, correctness, or creativity).

**Glare Sensitivity** (`q9yyh6jazh`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.4.a.7)  
The ability to see objects in the presence of a glare or bright lighting.

**Goal-oriented** (`g32rgj6uc5`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
A disposition to set clear goals, keep effort aimed at them, and judge work by progress toward them.

Flags: classification_dispute: A work-style disposition, not an ability; near-synonym of Results-oriented and Success-driven in job-listing use.

**Gross Body Coordination** (`wx2f68rm4b`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.3.c.3)  
The ability to coordinate the movement of your arms, legs, and torso together when the whole body is in motion.

**Gross Body Equilibrium** (`j2f27wcnfn`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.3.c.4)  
The ability to keep or regain your body balance or stay upright when in an unstable position.

**Hearing Sensitivity** (`huqfhwdt71`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.4.b.1)  
The ability to detect or tell the differences between sounds that vary in pitch and loudness.

**Inductive Reasoning** (`xk73ola6io`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.b.5)  
The ability to combine pieces of information to form general rules or conclusions (includes finding a relationship among seemingly unrelated events).

**Information Ordering** (`xw3kcuaybt`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.b.6)  
The ability to arrange things or actions in a certain order or pattern according to a specific rule or set of rules (e.g., patterns of numbers, letters, words, pictures, mathematical operations).

**Ingenuity** (`my2u4mc16o`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
The inventive capacity to devise clever, workable solutions or workarounds from limited means or in unfamiliar situations.

Relations: related → Originality (`yvb9iuakkb`)<br>Flags: classification_dispute: Overlaps the creativity-related ability Originality and is often a trait descriptor in listings; canonical class preserved.

**Manual Dexterity** (`jc7evcedfy`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.2.a.2)  
The ability to quickly move your hand, your hand together with your arm, or your two hands to grasp, manipulate, or assemble objects.

**Mathematical Reasoning** (`ec2b8ggmp3`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.c.1)  
The ability to choose the right mathematical methods or formulas to solve a problem.

**Memorization** (`jh2rauw9ao`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.d.1)  
The ability to remember information such as words, numbers, pictures, and procedures.

**Multilimb Coordination** (`oj8tgn6rrs`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.2.b.2)  
The ability to coordinate two or more limbs (for example, two arms, two legs, or one leg and one arm) while sitting, standing, or lying down. It does not involve performing the activities while the whole body is in motion.

**Near Vision** (`gr0hvlp5yb`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.4.a.1)  
The ability to see details at close range (within a few feet of the observer).

**Night Vision** (`jnssutztq4`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.4.a.4)  
The ability to see under low-light conditions.

**Number Facility** (`o37ow8vloe`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.c.2)  
The ability to add, subtract, multiply, or divide quickly and correctly.

**Oral Comprehension** (`l1hwn9qrn9`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.a.1)  
The ability to listen to and understand information and ideas presented through spoken words and sentences.

**Oral Expression** (`jw6x9szvrd`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.a.3)  
The ability to communicate information and ideas in speaking so others will understand.

**Originality** (`yvb9iuakkb`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.b.2)  
The ability to come up with unusual or clever ideas about a given topic or situation, or to develop creative ways to solve a problem.

**Patience** (`rrzqh6zhic`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
The disposition to stay calm and keep working courteously through delay, difficulty, repetition or slow progress by others without becoming irritated or rushing.

Flags: classification_dispute: A disposition or work style, not an ability.

**Perceptual Speed** (`sohvi65uoe`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.e.3)  
The ability to quickly and accurately compare similarities and differences among sets of letters, numbers, objects, pictures, or patterns. The things to be compared may be presented at the same time or one after the other. This ability also includes comparing a presented object with a remembered object.

**Peripheral Vision** (`ql1b59a2y6`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.4.a.5)  
The ability to see objects or movement of objects to one's side when the eyes are looking ahead.

**Problem Sensitivity** (`y2z5t8l784`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.b.3)  
The ability to tell when something is wrong or is likely to go wrong. It does not involve solving the problem, only recognizing that there is a problem.

**Quick learning** (`sg1xfslxld`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
The capacity to pick up new information, procedures or tools rapidly and apply them with little instruction or repetition.

Relations: related → Active Learning (`xubhw9lr07`); related → Learning Strategies (`ae15w1gozp`)<br>Flags: sense_note: Overlaps the O*NET skills Active Learning and Learning Strategies, which describe trained use; kept here as the underlying rate-of-learning attribute.

**Rate Control** (`srf5navpeq`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.2.b.4)  
The ability to time your movements or the movement of a piece of equipment in anticipation of changes in the speed and/or direction of a moving object or scene.

**Reaction Time** (`gg3liy57bv`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.2.c.1)  
The ability to quickly respond (with the hand, finger, or foot) to a signal (sound, light, picture) when it appears.

**Resilience** (`gov0d8zbms`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
The capacity to recover from setbacks, stress or failure and keep performing, adjusting approach rather than giving up.

Flags: classification_dispute: A disposition or personal characteristic; the class of other characteristics is not yet built in the source taxonomy.

**Response Orientation** (`crsg1gr1gz`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.2.b.3)  
The ability to choose quickly between two or more movements in response to two or more different signals (lights, sounds, pictures). It includes the speed with which the correct response is started with the hand, foot, or other body part.

**Results-oriented** (`id244h1e6v`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
A disposition to focus on achieving concrete, measurable outcomes and to prioritize effort by the results it produces.

Flags: classification_dispute: A work-style disposition, not an ability.

**Selective Attention** (`qhlfkhgfw3`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.g.1)  
The ability to concentrate on a task over a period of time without being distracted.

**Self-confidence** (`k2ayt41ale`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
A realistic belief in one's own capacity to handle tasks and challenges, shown by willingness to take on work, decide and speak up.

Flags: classification_dispute: A personal characteristic rather than an ability.

**Self-starter** (`hq2g370jgj`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
A disposition to begin and carry work forward on one's own initiative, without waiting for instruction or close supervision.

Flags: classification_dispute: A disposition, not an ability.

**Sound Localization** (`a15hsq0i5n`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.4.b.3)  
The ability to tell the direction from which a sound originated.

**Spatial Orientation** (`hctsy3pwrq`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.f.1)  
The ability to know your location in relation to the environment or to know where other objects are in relation to you.

**Spatial-temporal reasoning** (`my2r15ngyp`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
The cognitive capacity to mentally represent how objects, space and time relate and change, for example visualizing motion, sequences, rotations or layouts.

Relations: related → Spatial Orientation (`hctsy3pwrq`); related → Visualization (`xhr0najg6f`)<br>Flags: sense_note: Overlaps the O*NET abilities Spatial Orientation and Visualization; the temporal component is what distinguishes it.

**Speech Clarity** (`fnfui6ih6n`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.4.b.5)  
The ability to speak clearly so others can understand you.

**Speech Recognition** (`enx40y6s0v`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.4.b.4)  
The ability to identify and understand the speech of another person.

**Speed of Closure** (`q0u0azoaf6`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.e.1)  
The ability to quickly make sense of, combine, and organize information into meaningful patterns.

**Speed of Limb Movement** (`ckxpuw0cke`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.2.c.3)  
The ability to quickly move the arms and legs.

**Stamina** (`xcifis2ksa`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.3.b.1)  
The ability to exert yourself physically over long periods of time without getting winded or out of breath.

**Static Strength** (`hjh9isz4cp`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.3.a.1)  
The ability to exert maximum muscle force to lift, push, pull, or carry objects.

**Success-driven** (`uvzu3z28qp`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
A disposition marked by strong motivation to achieve, win or excel and to keep pushing for high performance.

Flags: classification_dispute: A motivational trait, not an ability; vague listing phrase close to Results-oriented and Goal-oriented.

**Team-oriented** (`crm3w31oq7`, ability; basis: model knowledge)  
A disposition to prefer collaborating with others, sharing credit and information, and putting team goals ahead of individual ones.

Relations: related → Teamwork (`tz7ipk5s96`)<br>Flags: classification_dispute: A work-style disposition; Teamwork is the corresponding developed skill.

**Time Sharing** (`t6ptjyq1kp`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.g.2)  
The ability to shift back and forth between two or more activities or sources of information (such as speech, sounds, touch, or other sources).

**Trunk Strength** (`q19rzqt2qa`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.3.a.4)  
The ability to use your abdominal and lower back muscles to support part of the body repeatedly or continuously over time without "giving out" or fatiguing.

**Visual Color Discrimination** (`zctapidef2`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.4.a.3)  
The ability to match or detect differences between colors, including shades of color and brightness.

**Visualization** (`xhr0najg6f`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.f.2)  
The ability to imagine how something will look after it is moved around or when its parts are moved or rearranged.

**Wrist-Finger Speed** (`aqqbcc2yzm`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.2.c.2)  
The ability to make fast, simple, repeated movements of the fingers, hands, and wrists.

**Written Comprehension** (`ts63qo7sda`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.a.2)  
The ability to read and understand information and ideas presented in writing.

**Written Expression** (`v2bj7va4w6`, ability; basis: O*NET 30.2 1.A.1.a.4)  
The ability to communicate information and ideas in writing so others will understand.

## Language

### Kind: human-language

**Afrikaans** (`wrombjtyy8`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A West Germanic language that developed from 17th-century Dutch in southern Africa; an official language of South Africa, also spoken in Namibia.

**Ainu** (`vks1qiiz57`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The language of the indigenous Ainu people of Hokkaido and nearby islands; unrelated to Japanese and critically endangered, with revival efforts under way.

**Akan** (`de1c4bnci7`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Kwa language of Ghana and Côte d'Ivoire made up of closely related varieties such as Twi and Fante; one of Ghana's most widely spoken languages.

**Albanian** (`p7j275wpwg`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The Indo-European language of Albania and Kosovo, forming its own branch of the family, with Gheg and Tosk dialect groups; the standard is based on Tosk.

**American Sign Language** (`l2yggbg9hw`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The natural sign language of the Deaf community in the United States and much of English-speaking Canada, with a grammar of its own distinct from English; used in interpreting, Deaf education and accessibility work.

Variants: ASL

**Amharic** (`eembc96hrl`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Semitic language and the working federal language of Ethiopia, written in an Ethiopic (Ge'ez-derived) script.

**Arabic** (`c6s0kc7cs8`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Semitic language of the Arab world and the liturgical language of Islam; Modern Standard Arabic serves writing and formal media while spoken regional varieties differ widely.

**Armenian** (`lc66pgzr2o`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The Indo-European language of Armenia, forming its own branch of the family and written in the Armenian alphabet; standard Eastern and Western forms exist.

**Assamese** (`nprk9hwd27`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
An Indo-Aryan language of Assam in northeastern India and the state's official language, written in a variant of the Bengali-Assamese script.

**Aymara** (`r46ots78g8`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
An Aymaran language of the Andean altiplano in Bolivia, Peru and Chile; official in Bolivia and Peru.

**Azerbaijani** (`vg1j943h99`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Turkic language of Azerbaijan and northwestern Iran, written in Latin script in Azerbaijan and in Perso-Arabic script in Iran.

Variants: Azeri

**Bassa** (`bzhvu8qe7g`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Kru language of Liberia spoken by the Bassa people, which has its own indigenous Vah script.

Flags: low_confidence: The label does not say whether it means the Liberian Kru language Bassa or the Bantu language Basaa of Cameroon; the Liberian language is assumed.

**Batak** (`xbe5epb1r9`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A group of related Austronesian languages of northern Sumatra, Indonesia, including Toba, Karo, Simalungun and Mandailing, historically written in a Batak script.

Flags: sense_note: The label names a cluster of languages rather than one language.

**Bemba** (`h0wn66qxrk`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Bantu language of northern Zambia and neighboring areas, widely used as a regional lingua franca in Zambia.

**Benga** (`ynw7yrzobc`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A small Bantu language of coastal Equatorial Guinea and Gabon.

**Bengali** (`k87r3b7xk8`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
An Indo-Aryan language of Bangladesh and the Indian state of West Bengal, the national language of Bangladesh, written in the Bengali script.

Variants: Bangla

**Berber** (`wyc8jqdxhm`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The family of Afroasiatic languages indigenous to North Africa, including Tashelhit, Kabyle, Tamazight and Tuareg varieties.

Variants: Amazigh<br>Flags: sense_note: The label names a language family; work listings usually mean a specific variety such as Tashelhit or Kabyle.

**Bislama** (`kv7ufmoq1h`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
An English-based creole and national language of Vanuatu, used as a lingua franca among the country's many local languages.

**Bosnian** (`t86zp83kn8`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A South Slavic language standard used chiefly by Bosniaks in Bosnia and Herzegovina, mutually intelligible with Croatian and Serbian.

Relations: related → Serbo-Croatian (`zrvz05z4iv`)

**Burmese** (`xzacqgvx93`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The Sino-Tibetan language and official language of Myanmar, written in the Burmese script.

Variants: Myanmar language

**Carib** (`qlaxx8o2dm`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Cariban language, also called Kari'nja, spoken by the Carib people along the northern coast of South America in Suriname, Guyana, French Guiana, Venezuela and Brazil.

Flags: sense_note: The label may also be read as the wider Carib language family or as the Antillean language historically called Island Carib.

**Castilian Spanish** (`n2zf1jjewl`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The Spanish of Castile, taken as the standard form in Spain and often used as another name for Spanish in contrast to other Spanish-language varieties and regional languages of Spain.

Relations: related → Spanish (`de8aeltv9z`)<br>Flags: sense_note: Likely an alias or regional label for Spanish rather than a separate language; kept separate by ID as in the roster.

**Catalan** (`b53t6y9iys`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Romance language of Catalonia, Valencia, the Balearic Islands, Andorra and nearby areas; official in Andorra and co-official in several regions of Spain.

**Cebuano** (`ms42m7io2w`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
An Austronesian language of the central Philippines, widely spoken in Cebu, Bohol, eastern Negros and parts of Mindanao.

**Chakma** (`mrfo4d84xg`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
An Indo-Aryan language of the Chakma people in the Chittagong Hill Tracts of Bangladesh and in northeastern India, traditionally written in its own script.

**Chamorro** (`r5qkbzvx25`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
An Austronesian language of Guam and the Northern Mariana Islands, official there alongside English and rich in Spanish loan vocabulary.

**Chichewa** (`nbdd2ehtfg`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Bantu language of Malawi and neighboring parts of Zambia, Mozambique and Zimbabwe; the national language of Malawi.

Variants: Nyanja; Chewa

**Chinese** (`hj8i6mh3zz`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The family of Sinitic languages of the Han Chinese, including Mandarin, Cantonese, Wu and Min, which share a writing system of Chinese characters.

Relations: related → Mandarin (`vphlc8dpfb`)<br>Flags: sense_note: The label names a family; in work listings it usually means Mandarin, written in simplified or traditional characters.

**Czech** (`jozc69fgpa`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A West Slavic language of the Czech Republic, written in the Latin alphabet with diacritics.

**Danish** (`x09au1cwqd`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A North Germanic language of Denmark, also spoken in Greenland and the Faroe Islands and partly intelligible with Norwegian and Swedish.

Relations: related → Norwegian (`ez2j813g66`); related → Swedish (`lr6v3jj0ke`)

**Dari** (`ar6o7kobv7`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The variety of Persian that is an official language of Afghanistan, also called Afghan Persian.

Variants: Afghan Persian<br>Relations: broader → Persian (`kubq5ue8ww`)

**Dhivehi** (`s7rg5fey2w`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
An Indo-Aryan language and national language of the Maldives, written right to left in the Thaana script.

**Dinka** (`pak2r3gski`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Nilotic language cluster of South Sudan spoken by the Dinka people, with several dialects that differ considerably.

**Duala** (`l6le846miv`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Bantu language of the Duala people of coastal Cameroon, historically a trade language around the Wouri estuary.

Variants: Douala

**Dutch** (`ol3tcjafkq`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A West Germanic language of the Netherlands and Flanders in Belgium, and an official language of Suriname.

**Dzongkha** (`zx3or51hr1`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The Tibetic national language of Bhutan, written in the Tibetan script.

**English** (`tsdlbd9gt0`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A West Germanic language that serves as a leading international language of business, science, aviation and the internet, with national standards such as American and British English.

**Ewondo** (`wxuml71bfu`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Bantu language of the Beti group spoken in the Yaoundé region of southern Cameroon.

**Fali** (`q0vx2lgkj8`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A name used for the language or languages of the Fali people of northern Cameroon and northeastern Nigeria.

Flags: low_confidence: Several distinct Adamawa languages are called Fali and the label does not say which; the definition is deliberately minimal.

**Filipino** (`dn5iveu53x`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The national language of the Philippines, a standardized register based on Tagalog and enriched with words from other Philippine languages, Spanish and English.

Variants: Pilipino

**Finnish** (`q98sd6zk57`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Uralic language of Finland, agglutinative with many noun cases and unrelated to the Indo-European languages around it; official in Finland alongside Swedish.

**French** (`jeai1j8uhz`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Romance language spoken on several continents and an official language in many countries and international bodies such as the United Nations and the European Union.

**Frisian** (`uj60c4yrr3`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A group of West Germanic languages of the Netherlands, Germany and Denmark, of which West Frisian in the Dutch province of Friesland is the most widely spoken.

**Fula** (`z199mo1bdd`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Niger-Congo language of the Fulani people spoken in a belt across West and Central Africa from Senegal to Cameroon.

Variants: Fulfulde; Pulaar

**Gaelic** (`goirkloj73`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Goidelic Celtic language of Scotland (Scottish Gaelic), or more loosely the Goidelic branch that also includes Irish and Manx.

Flags: ambiguous_label: The label can mean Scottish Gaelic, Irish or Manx; the intended language is not specified.

**Galo** (`c34c0nwfxj`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Tani language of the Sino-Tibetan family spoken by the Galo people of Arunachal Pradesh in northeastern India.

**Garifuna** (`os4bq6o3n0`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
An Arawakan language of the Garifuna people on the Caribbean coast of Honduras, Belize, Guatemala and Nicaragua.

**German** (`ww6z5fppct`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A West Germanic language of Germany, Austria and much of Switzerland, widely used in business, engineering and science; standard German coexists with regional dialects.

**Gikuyu** (`o410b5qw7m`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A Bantu language of the Kikuyu people of central Kenya, the first language of Kenya's largest ethnic group.

Variants: Kikuyu

**Greek** (`eon43rmtrr`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The Indo-European language of Greece and Cyprus, written in the Greek alphabet; Modern Greek descends from Ancient Greek.

Flags: sense_note: Presumed to mean Modern Greek unless a listing says Ancient or Koine Greek.

**Gujarati** (`hth5eczuzx`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
An Indo-Aryan language of the Indian state of Gujarat, written in the Gujarati script and widely spoken in diaspora communities.

**Haitian Creole** (`z2kugvplb2`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A French-based creole that is the national language of Haiti and the first language of most Haitians.

Variants: Kreyòl ayisyen

**Hausa** (`v5s8v3c7qh`, human-language; basis: model knowledge)

### Kind: programming-language

**ABAP** (`xiiyhhzd8l`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
SAP's proprietary programming language for writing reports, interfaces, extensions and applications on the SAP application server; developers use it to implement business logic and read and update SAP data.

Variants: Advanced Business Application Programming

**ActionScript** (`ayuiek7so7`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
An object-oriented scripting language derived from ECMAScript, used with Adobe Flash and AIR to build interactive animations, games and rich internet applications.

**Ada** (`x80b957frq`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A statically and strongly typed language designed for reliable, long-lived embedded and safety-critical systems in aviation, defense and rail, with built-in tasking for concurrency.

**Ada 95** (`k8pcmxo14a`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The 1995 revision of Ada (ISO/IEC 8652:1995), which added object-oriented programming, protected types and hierarchical libraries to Ada 83.

Relations: broader → Ada (`x80b957frq`)

**Algebraic Logic Functional** (`tfv49p04re`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A research language, known as ALF, that integrates functional and logic programming through equational definitions and logic-style search.

Variants: ALF<br>Flags: low_confidence: A niche research language identified from memory; the definition is deliberately brief.

**ALGOL** (`k29flhnozy`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A family of early imperative languages, including ALGOL 60 and ALGOL 68, that introduced block structure and formal grammar notation and shaped Pascal, C and later languages; used in algorithm publication and teaching.

**AMPscript** (`tl1644tlhi`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
Salesforce Marketing Cloud's scripting language embedded in emails, landing pages and messages to personalize content, look up data and apply conditional logic.

**Apex** (`ed3v3ccr0e`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
Salesforce's strongly typed, Java-like language that runs on the Salesforce platform; developers write triggers, classes and batch jobs to implement business logic and integrations.

**Assembly language** (`dwchdo7ntf`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A low-level language of mnemonics that map nearly one-to-one to a processor's machine instructions, used for firmware, drivers, performance-critical routines, reverse engineering and learning computer architecture.

Variants: Assembler

**AWK** (`znqe62lhes`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A small text-processing language that applies pattern-action rules to lines and fields of input, used on the command line to filter, summarize and reformat text and logs.

**Ballerina** (`soe310iks3`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A cloud-native, statically typed language with built-in support for network services, JSON and concurrency, used to write integrations and API services.

**Base SAS** (`zam3lqxfdr`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The core SAS programming language, with its data step and procedures, used to read, clean, transform, analyze and report data in the SAS system.

**Bash** (`op6iieveb7`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
The GNU Bourne-Again shell command language, used to run commands interactively and to write scripts that automate file handling, builds, deployments and administration on Unix-like systems.

**BASIC** (`gk12ir4ef9`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A family of beginner-friendly imperative languages dating from 1964, once bundled with microcomputers and now mostly used in teaching and legacy maintenance.

**BeanShell** (`kq6noua17o`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A small Java source interpreter and scripting language that runs Java-like code dynamically, used to embed scripting in Java applications and test tools.

**BlitzMax** (`oyo6sqevit`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A BASIC-derived, object-oriented compiled language aimed at 2D game and multimedia programming.

**Boo** (`qfyv8hdypt`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A statically typed, Python-inspired language for the .NET and Mono runtimes, once supported as a Unity scripting language.

**C** (`a0h79b66kq`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A low-level, general-purpose procedural language with manual memory management and close hardware access, used for operating systems, embedded firmware, drivers and performance-critical libraries.

**C#** (`dbxqhrpv38`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
Microsoft's object-oriented, statically typed language for the .NET platform, used for web services, desktop applications, games and enterprise software.

Variants: C Sharp

**C++** (`qb4qnsutxp`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A compiled, multi-paradigm extension of C with classes and templates, used for systems software, game engines, trading systems and other performance-critical applications.

Relations: related → C (`a0h79b66kq`)

**CFML** (`gvutm225wm`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
ColdFusion Markup Language, the tag-and-script language of Adobe ColdFusion and Lucee used to build dynamic web pages, database-driven applications and web services on the JVM.

**Chipmunk Basic** (`emsn9xnsir`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A BASIC interpreter for macOS, Linux and Windows that runs classic BASIC programs, used for teaching, hobby programming and legacy code.

**CLIST** (`r9tfnc7x3j`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
Command List, an interpreted scripting language for IBM TSO on z/OS used to automate command sequences and dialogs, largely superseded by REXX.

**Clojure** (`hwas4qmror`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)  
A modern Lisp for the JVM that emphasizes immutable data, functional programming and concurrency, used for data processing and web back ends.

**COBOL** (`rw1cn65xyv`, programming-language; basis: model knowledge)

## Knowledge

### Kind: knowledge

**3D Touch** (`jx7it8oo69`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
Apple's pressure-sensitive touch technology for iPhone that distinguished light taps from deeper presses to trigger shortcuts and previews; knowledge of it is now needed mainly for legacy iOS interfaces.

**ABEC scale** (`x1ik9vrk6z`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The Annular Bearing Engineers' Committee ratings (ABEC 1, 3, 5, 7, 9) that grade the dimensional tolerances of precision ball bearings, used to judge a bearing's precision for an application.

Flags: classification_dispute: A rating standard rather than a field of knowledge.

**Access point name** (`dcojl2vxs3`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
In cellular networks, the identifier naming the gateway a mobile device uses to reach a data network, together with its settings.

Variants: APN

**Accumulated benefit obligation** (`uga6m4y1c2`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
In pension accounting, the present value of retirement benefits earned to date, measured on current salaries without projecting future pay increases.

**Action for Climate Empowerment** (`nqi6z4463l`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The UN climate convention's term for work on climate education, training, public awareness, public participation and access to information.

Variants: ACE

**Active pharmaceutical ingredient** (`kaq3khj4zt`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The component of a drug product that produces its intended therapeutic effect, with the principles of its manufacture, specification and quality control.

Variants: API

**Activities of daily living** (`kh1pwxmv04`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
Basic self-care tasks such as bathing, dressing, eating, toileting and moving about, and the assessment of a person's ability to perform them, used in nursing, occupational therapy and eldercare.

Variants: ADLs

**Actuarial exams** (`gd6cpzm0po`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The professional examination programs for actuaries, which test probability, financial mathematics, life contingencies, risk and related topics.

**Administration and Management** (`q7spu83n7z`, knowledge; basis: O*NET 30.2 2.C.1.a)  
Knowledge of business and management principles involved in strategic planning, resource allocation, human resources modeling, leadership technique, production methods, and coordination of people and resources.

**Administrative** (`v17l7v4fgv`, knowledge; basis: O*NET 30.2 2.C.1.b)  
Knowledge of administrative and office procedures and systems such as word processing, managing files and records, stenography and transcription, designing forms, and workplace terminology.

**Administrative law** (`d0ayx8tpi3`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The body of law governing how government agencies make rules, adjudicate disputes and are reviewed by courts.

**Advanced Automatic Collision Notification** (`ixo5hey83a`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
A vehicle telematics capability that automatically sends crash data to emergency services and estimates likely injury severity, used in emergency response and automotive safety.

Variants: AACN

**Airworthiness engineering** (`t1zvd2cmly`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The discipline of demonstrating and maintaining that aircraft design, production and maintenance meet certification requirements for safe flight.

**Analogue electronics** (`l9skd3jhai`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The principles of circuits that process continuously varying signals, such as amplifiers, filters, oscillators and power supplies.

**Ancient history** (`sjmv9bjgf0`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The study of human societies from the earliest records through late antiquity, including Mesopotamia, Egypt, Greece and Rome, based on texts and archaeology.

**Ancient philosophy** (`fkdu4zhgon`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The study of philosophical thought in ancient Greece and Rome, from the Presocratics, Plato and Aristotle to the Hellenistic schools.

**Andragogy** (`kii00iund2`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The theory and practice of teaching adults, built on ideas such as self-direction, prior experience and problem-centered learning.

**Animal science** (`u4n357y1ti`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The science of breeding, nutrition, physiology, health and management of livestock and other domestic animals.

**Anthropometrics** (`efwtd5psz9`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The measurement of human body dimensions and proportions, used in ergonomics, product and clothing design and health assessment.

**Anti-bribery and corruption** (`ao4kld81ar`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
Understanding of the laws, such as the US Foreign Corrupt Practices Act and the UK Bribery Act, and the compliance controls used to prevent, detect and respond to bribery and corruption.

**Application controls** (`aupdc445tz`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
Controls built into software to ensure that inputs, processing and outputs are complete, accurate and authorized, a core subject in IT auditing.

**Application delivery network** (`shrj08s20e`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The group of technologies, such as load balancers, caches and WAN optimizers, that speed up and secure delivery of applications to users.

Variants: ADN

**Application pool** (`wjdoqpvi1k`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
In Microsoft IIS, a group of one or more web applications that share a worker process, isolating applications for reliability and security.

**Application programming interface** (`xe2d2jfa1r`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
A defined contract of requests and responses that lets one piece of software use another's functions or data, including how APIs are designed, documented and consumed.

Variants: API

**Application-specific integrated circuit** (`hazuxnf4z3`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
A chip designed for a particular function or product rather than for general use, and the design, fabrication and cost trade-offs involved.

Variants: ASIC

**Applied linguistics** (`hm380yneeh`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The study of language as it relates to real-world problems such as language teaching, translation, assessment and language policy.

**Applied statistics** (`ww4glmysl2`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The use of statistical methods to collect, analyze and interpret data in real settings such as business, science and health.

**Archaeology** (`hmn1b2xrbu`, knowledge; basis: model knowledge)  
The study of past human societies through their material remains, using excavation, dating and analysis of artifacts and sites.
